- **graph**: Run The Graph Blueprint over a question or investigation — define the goal, split it into independent pieces, fan out parallel worker agents with different angles (research, compare, check, find gaps), verify the results with a fresh-context verifier, merge what survives, and deliver one clear actionable report. Use when the user invokes /graph, asks for a multi-angle or parallel investigation, or a broad question would overload a single linear pass. ([rkmax/claude-plugins](https://github.com/rkmax/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/graph/skills/graph))
- **graph**: The book graph — characters, organizations, objects, places, concepts, terms and events (card types depend on the genre preset), linked to each other and to the exact line of the chapter they come from. Feeds itself: only chapters with `status: complete` enter it, and it re-syncs whenever a complete chapter changes version. Other skills call its Sync procedure; run it by hand only to look something up, to force a sync, or to migrate an old graph. ([simonediroma/claude-ghost-writer](https://github.com/simonediroma/claude-ghost-writer/tree/HEAD/skills/graph))
- **graph**: Open an interactive visualization of plasticity long-term memory (the knowledge graph) in the browser ([vhavlena/plasticity](https://github.com/vhavlena/plasticity/tree/HEAD/skills/graph))
- **Graph Analysis**: Analyse patterns, connections and clusters in the knowledge graph to surface insights about relationships between entities. ([jakobengdahl/CommunityOverview](https://github.com/jakobengdahl/CommunityOverview/tree/HEAD/config/stat-metadata/skills/graph-analysis) · ★3)
- **Graph Analytics**: Análise de grafo — Cross-Memory Synthesis (clustering por similaridade), Graph Clustering (comunidades via Cypher), Weight Propagation (PageRank alternativo), e Matryoshka multi-resolution search. ([Renzo-Tognella/UniversalThingsForMyAgents](https://github.com/Renzo-Tognella/UniversalThingsForMyAgents/tree/HEAD/skills/41_graph_analytics))
- **Graph Engineering**: Design resilient multi-step LLM/agent workflows using the loops-and-graphs pattern. Covers all 5 layers: reflection, tool use, planning, multi-agent coordination, and critique loops — with anti-patterns and cost guidance. ([BhaveshKhaple/bhavesh-claude-skills](https://github.com/BhaveshKhaple/bhavesh-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/graph-engineering) · ★1)
- **Graph functions with integer-coordinate points**: Identify and list points with integer coordinates for a given mathematical function, ensuring they fit within specified x and y axis ranges (e.g., -10 to 10). ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/ConvSkill/english_gpt4_8/graph-functions-with-integer-coordinate-points) · ★620 · 4 copies)
- **Graph Memory Architecture**: Use when modifying the Neo4j graph model, adding node/relationship types, implementing Personalized PageRank retrieval, hierarchical context trees, spreading activation, or community detection for memory. ([Renzo-Tognella/UniversalThingsForMyAgents](https://github.com/Renzo-Tognella/UniversalThingsForMyAgents/tree/HEAD/skills/21_graph_memory_architecture))
- **Graph Theory**: Graph algorithms including shortest paths, network flow, matching, connectivity, coloring, and community detection for network analysis. ([ffsshhttiikk/opencode-agents-skills](https://github.com/ffsshhttiikk/opencode-agents-skills/tree/HEAD/graph-theory) · ★3 · 3 copies)
